<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Joshua 23:1–2; 1 Chronicles 29:27–28; 1 Chronicles 23:1"> Now king David &#91;beloved&#93; was old &#91;and&#93; stricken in years; and they covered him with clothes, but he got no heat.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 4:11; Genesis 16:5; Micah 7:5"> For what reason his servants said unto him, Let there be sought for my lord the king a young virgin: and let her stand before the king, and let her cherish him, and let her lie in youri bosom, that my lord the king may get heat.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="2 Samuel 15:1; 2 Samuel 3:4; Deuteronomy 17:15–16"> In Giv’on &#91;Hill City&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) appeared to Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; in a dream by night: and God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; said, Ask what I shall give youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Samuel 3:3–4; 1 Samuel 3:13; Hebrews 12:5–6"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; said, youi have showed unto youi servant David &#91;beloved&#93; my father great mercy, according as he walked before youi in truth, and in righteousness, and in uprightness of heart with youi; and youi have kept for him this great kindness, that youi have given him a son to sit on his throne, as &#91;it is&#93; this day.</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Kings 4:18; 2 Samuel 23:8–39; 2 Samuel 20:25"> And youri servant &#91;is&#93; in the midst of youri people which youi have chosen, a great people, that cannot be numbered nor counted for multitude.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="2 Samuel 12:24; 1 Kings 1:19; 2 Samuel 12:1–15"> And the speech pleased the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, that Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; had asked this thing.</span>

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="1 Kings 1:25; 1 Kings 1:7–10"> And this woman’s child died in the night; because she overlaid it.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="1 Kings 1:16; 1 Peter 2:17; Romans 13:7"> Then said the king, The one says, This &#91;is&#93; my son that lives, and youri son &#91;is&#93; the dead: and the other says, No; but youri son &#91;is&#93; the dead, and my son &#91;is&#93; the living.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Gosel 1:25"> But now the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has given me rest on every side, &#91;so that there is&#93; neither adversary nor evil evident.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Samuel 3:3–4; 1 Samuel 3:13; Hebrews 12:5–6"> The nethermost chamber &#91;was&#93; five cubits broad, and the middle &#91;was&#93; six cubits broad, and the third &#91;was&#93; seven cubits broad: for without &#91;in the wall&#93; of the house he made narrowed rests round about, that &#91;the beams&#93; should not be fastened in the walls of the house.</span>

<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Kings 4:18; 2 Samuel 23:8–39; 2 Samuel 20:25"> The door for the middle chamber &#91;was&#93; in the right side of the house: and they went up with winding stairs into the middle &#91;chamber&#93;, and out of the middle into the third.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 11:14; Acts 27:31; Genesis 19:17"> &#91;Concerning&#93; this house which youi are in building, if youi will walk in my statutes, and execute my judgments, and keep all my commandments to walk in them; then will I perform my word with youi, which I spoke unto David &#91;beloved&#93; youi father:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="1 Kings 1:1"> And he built the walls of the house within with boards of cedar, both the floor of the house, and the walls of the ceiling: &#91;and&#93; he covered &#91;them&#93; on the inside with wood, and covered the floor of the house with planks of fir.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="1 Samuel 25:23; 1 Samuel 24:8; 1 Samuel 20:41"> And he built twenty cubits on the sides of the house, both the floor and the walls with boards of cedar: he even built &#91;them&#93; for it within, &#91;even&#93; for the oracle, &#91;even&#93; for the most holy &#91;place&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>

And he came to king Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, and wrought all his work.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="1 Kings 1:1"> For he cast two pillars of brass, of eighteen cubits high apiece: and a line of twelve cubits did compass (go around, encircle; encompass) either of them about.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:23; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11; 1 Kings 1:13"> In the plain of Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; did the king cast them, in the clay ground between Sukkot &#91;Booths&#93; and Tzartan &#91;their distress&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47" data-xref="Genesis 47:31; 1 Kings 1:37; Ezra 6:10"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; left all the vessels &#91;unweighed&#93;, because they were exceeding many: neither was the weight of the brass found out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48" data-xref="1 Kings 3:6; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 145:2"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; made all the vessels that &#91;pertained&#93; unto the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): the altar of gold, and the table of gold, whereupon the showbread &#91;was&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49" data-xref="Isaiah 21:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 28:1; Daniel 5:4–6"> And the candlesticks of pure gold, five on the right &#91;side&#93;, and five on the left, before the oracle, with the flowers, and the lamps, and the tongs &#91;of&#93; gold,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50" data-xref="1 Kings 2:28; Exodus 21:14; Exodus 27:2"> And the bowls, and the snuffers, and the basins, and the spoons, and the censers &#91;of&#93; pure gold; and the hinges &#91;of&#93; gold, &#91;both&#93; for the doors of the inner house, the most holy &#91;place, and&#93; for the doors of the house, &#91;that is to say&#93;, of the temple.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> So was ended all the work that king Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; made for the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; brought in the things which David &#91;beloved&#93; his father had dedicated; &#91;even&#93; the silver, and the gold, and the vessels, did he put among the treasures of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Joshua 23:1–2; 1 Chronicles 29:27–28; 1 Chronicles 23:1"> Then Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; assembled the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and all the heads of the tribes, the chief of the fathers of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, unto king Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, that they might bring up the ark of the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) out of the city of David &#91;beloved&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 4:11; Genesis 16:5; Micah 7:5"> And all the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; assembled themselves unto king Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; at the feast in the month Etanim &#91;enduring&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; the seventh month.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Joshua 19:18; Ester 2:4; Ester 2:2"> And all the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; came, and the priests took up the ark.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Gosel 1:25"> And they brought up the ark of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and the tabernacle of the congregation, and all the holy vessels that &#91;were&#93; in the tabernacle, even those did the priests and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; bring up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="2 Samuel 15:1; 2 Samuel 3:4; Deuteronomy 17:15–16"> And king Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, and all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that were assembled unto him, &#91;were&#93; with him before the ark, sacrificing sheep and oxen, that could not be told nor numbered for multitude.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Samuel 3:3–4; 1 Samuel 3:13; Hebrews 12:5–6"> And the priests brought in the ark of the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto his place, into the oracle of the house, to the most holy &#91;place, even&#93; under the wings of the k’vurim (cherubim).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="2 Samuel 20:25; 1 Kings 2:22; 1 Samuel 22:20–23"> For the k’vurim (cherubim) spread forth &#91;their&#93; two wings over the place of the ark, and the k’vurim (cherubim) covered the ark and the rods (clubs) thereof above.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Kings 4:18; 2 Samuel 23:8–39; 2 Samuel 20:25"> And they drew out the rods (clubs), that the ends of the rods (clubs) were seen out in the holy &#91;place&#93; before the oracle, and they were not seen without: and there they are unto this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="2 Samuel 17:17; 2 Samuel 13:23–27; 2 Samuel 15:11–12"> &#91;There was&#93; nothing in the ark save the two tables of stone, which Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; put there at Horev &#91;Desert&#93;, when the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) made &#91;a covenant&#93; with the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when they came out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="2 Samuel 12:24; 1 Kings 1:19; 2 Samuel 12:1–15"> And it came to pass, when the priests were come out of the holy &#91;place&#93;, that the cloud filled the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate),</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="2 Samuel 3:4; 2 Samuel 7:12–17; 2 Samuel 12:24–25"> So that the priests could not stand to minister because of the cloud: for the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had filled the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Mishlei - Proverbs 11:14; Acts 27:31; Genesis 19:17"> Then spoke Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said that he would dwell in the thick darkness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="1 Kings 1:30; 1 Kings 1:17; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12"> I have surely built youi an house to dwell in, a settled place for youi to abide in forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="2 Corinthians 13:1; 1 Kings 1:17–27"> And the king turned his face about, and Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; (and all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; stood;)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="1 Kings 1:1"> And he said, Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which spoke with his mouth unto David &#91;beloved&#93; my father, and has with his hand fulfilled &#91;it&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="1 Samuel 25:23; 1 Samuel 24:8; 1 Samuel 20:41"> Since the day that I brought forth my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, I chose no city out of all the tribes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to build an house, that my name might be in it; but I chose David &#91;beloved&#93; to be over my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="1 Kings 1:13; 1 Kings 1:30; 1 Peter 3:6"> And it was in the heart of David &#91;beloved&#93; my father to build an house for the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="1 Kings 1:24; 1 Kings 1:11; Acts 3:17"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto David &#91;beloved&#93; my father, Whereas it was in youi heart to build an house unto my name, youi did well that it was in youi heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="1 Kings 1:25; 1 Kings 1:7–10"> Nevertheless youi shall not build the house; but youri son that shall come forth out of youri waist, he shall build the house unto my name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:1; Tehillim - Psalms 123:2; 1 Chronicles 22:8–10"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has performed his word that he spoke, and I am risen up in the room of David &#91;beloved&#93; my father, and sit on the throne of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) promised, and have built an house for the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 2:10; Deuteronomy 31:16; Genesis 15:15"> And I have set there a place for the ark, wherein &#91;is&#93; the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), which he made with our fathers, when he brought them out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Job 1:16–18; Genesis 24:15; Daniel 9:20"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; stood before the altar of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the presence of all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and spread forth his hands toward heaven:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="1 Kings 1:16; 1 Peter 2:17; Romans 13:7"> And he said, LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, &#91;there is&#93; no God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; like youi, in heaven above, or on earth beneath, who keeps covenant and mercy with youi servants that walk before youi with all their heart:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="1 Kings 1:17–18; 1 Kings 1:5; 1 Kings 1:13–14"> Who have kept with youri servant David &#91;beloved&#93; my father that youi promised him: youi spoke also with youri mouth, and have fulfilled &#91;it&#93; with youri hand, as &#91;it is&#93; this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="1 Samuel 10:24; 1 Kings 1:19; 1 Kings 1:9"> Therefore now, LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, keep with youri servant David &#91;beloved&#93; my father that youi promised him, saying, There shall not fail youi a man in my sight to sit on the throne of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; so that youri children take heed to their way, that they walk before me as youi have walked before me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="1 Kings 1:8; 1 Kings 1:10; 2 Samuel 7:2"> And now, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, let youi word, I pray youi, be verified, which youi spoke unto youri servant David &#91;beloved&#93; my father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="John 15:15; 1 Kings 1:24; 2 Kings 4:27"> But will God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; indeed dwell on the earth? behold, the heaven and heaven of heavens cannot contain youi; how much less this house that I have built?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Yet have youi respect unto the prayer of youri servant, and to his supplication, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, to hearken unto the cry and to the prayer, which youri servant prays before youi today:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="2 Samuel 4:9; Tehillim - Psalms 136:24; 1 Samuel 14:45"> That youri eyes may be open toward this house night and day, &#91;even&#93; toward the place of which youi have said, My name shall be there: that youi may hearken unto the prayer which youri servant shall make toward this place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="1 Kings 1:13; 1 Kings 1:17"> And hearken youi to the supplication of youri servant, and of youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when they shall pray toward this place: and hear youi in heaven youri dwelling place: and when youi hear, forgive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Daniel 2:4; Daniel 5:10; Daniel 3:9"> If any man trespass against his neighbour, and an oath be laid upon him to cause him to swear, and the oath come before youri altar in this house:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="1 Kings 1:8; 1 Kings 1:38; 1 Kings 1:26"> Then hear youi in heaven, and do, and judge youri servants, condemning the wicked, to bring his way upon his head; and justifying the righteous, to give him according to his righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="2 Chronicles 32:30; 1 Kings 1:38; 2 Chronicles 33:14"> When youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; be smitten down before the enemy, because they have sinned against youi, and shall turn again to youi, and confess youri name, and pray, and make supplication unto youi in this house:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="1 Kings 1:25; 2 Samuel 15:10; 1 Samuel 10:1"> Then hear youi in heaven, and forgive the sin of youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and bring them again unto the land which youi gave unto their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="1 Kings 1:17; Tehillim - Psalms 72:1–2; 1 Kings 1:13"> When heaven is shut up, and there is no rain, because they have sinned against youi; if they pray toward this place, and confess youri name, and turn from their sin, when youi afflict them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36" data-xref="Jeremiah 28:6; 1 Corinthians 14:16; Tehillim - Psalms 72:19"> Then hear youi in heaven, and forgive the sin of youri servants, and of youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that youi teach them the good way wherein they should walk, and give rain upon youri land, which youi have given to youri people for an inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37" data-xref="1 Samuel 20:13; 1 Kings 1:47; Joshua 1:17"> If there be in the land famine, if there be pestilence, blasting, mildew, locust, &#91;or&#93; if there be caterpillar; if their enemy besiege them in the land of their cities; whatsoever plague, whatsoever sickness &#91;there be&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="2 Samuel 8:18; 1 Kings 1:8; 1 Kings 1:33"> What prayer and supplication soever be &#91;made&#93; by any man, &#91;or&#93; by all youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which shall know every man the plague of his own heart, and spread forth his hands toward this house:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 89:20; 1 Kings 1:34; 1 Chronicles 29:22"> Then hear youi in heaven youri dwelling place, and forgive, and do, and give to every man according to his ways, whose heart youi know; (for youi, &#91;even&#93; youi only, know the hearts of all the children of men;)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="2 Kings 11:14; Zechariah 9:9; 2 Kings 11:20"> That they may fear youi all the days that they live in the land which youi gave unto our fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Job 15:21–22; Luke 17:26–29; Acts 21:31"> Moreover concerning a stranger, that &#91;is&#93; not of youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, but comes out of a far country for youri name’s sake;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42" data-xref="2 Samuel 15:36; 2 Samuel 17:17; 2 Samuel 18:27"> (For they shall hear of youri great name, and of youi strong hand, and of youri stretched out arm;) when he shall come and pray toward this house;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> Hear youi in heaven youri dwelling place, and do according to all that the stranger calls to youi for: that all people of the earth may know youri name, to fear youi, as &#91;do&#93; youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and that they may know that this house, which I have built, is called by youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> If youri people go out to battle against their enemy, to wherever youi shall send them, and shall pray unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) toward the city which youi have chosen, and &#91;toward&#93; the house that I have built for youri name:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45" data-xref="1 Kings 1:40; Daniel 5:26–28; 1 Samuel 4:5"> Then hear youi in heaven their prayer and their supplication, and maintain their cause.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:23; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11; 1 Kings 1:13"> If they sin against youi, (for &#91;there is&#93; no man that sins not,) and youi be angry with them, and deliver them to the enemy, so that they carry them away captives unto the land of the enemy, far or near;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47" data-xref="Genesis 47:31; 1 Kings 1:37; Ezra 6:10"> &#91;Yet&#93; if they shall consider themselves in the land to which they were carried captives, and repent, and make supplication unto youi in the land of them that carried them captives, saying, We have sinned, and have done perversely, we have committed wickedness;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48" data-xref="1 Kings 3:6; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 145:2"> And &#91;so&#93; return unto youi with all their heart, and with all their soul, in the land of their enemies, which led them away captive, and pray unto youi toward their land, which youi gave unto their fathers, the city which youi have chosen, and the house which I have built for youri name:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49" data-xref="Isaiah 21:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 28:1; Daniel 5:4–6"> Then hear youi their prayer and their supplication in heaven youri dwelling place, and maintain their cause,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50" data-xref="1 Kings 2:28; Exodus 21:14; Exodus 27:2"> And forgive youri people that have sinned against youi, and all their transgressions wherein they have transgressed against youi, and give them compassion before them who carried them captive, that they may have compassion on them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> For they &#91;be&#93; youri people, and youri inheritance, which youi brought forth out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, from the midst of the furnace of iron:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52" data-xref="1 Samuel 14:45; 2 Samuel 14:11; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:6"> That youri eyes may be open unto the supplication of youri servant, and unto the supplication of youri people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, to hearken unto them in all that they call for unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53" data-xref="1 Kings 1:16; 2 Samuel 14:24; 2 Samuel 1:2"> For youi did separate them from among all the people of the earth, &#91;to be&#93; youri inheritance, as youi spoke by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; youri servant, when youi brought our fathers out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54"> And it was &#91;so&#93;, that when Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; had made an end of praying all this prayer and supplication unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), he arose from before the altar of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), from kneeling on his knees with his hands spread up to heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> And he stood, and Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; with a loud voice, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that has given rest unto his people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, according to all that he promised: there has not failed one word of all his good promise, which he promised by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; his servant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v57" data-v="57"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; be with us, as he was with our fathers: let him not leave us, nor forsake us:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v58" data-v="58"> That he may incline our hearts unto him, to walk in all his ways, and to keep his commandments, and his statutes, and his judgments, which he commanded our fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v59" data-v="59"> And let these my words, which I have made supplication before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), be near unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; day and night, that he maintain the cause of his servant, and the cause of his people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; at all times, as the matter shall require:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v60" data-v="60"> That all the people of the earth may know that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;is&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;and that there is&#93; none else.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v61" data-v="61"> Let your heart therefore be perfect with the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, to walk in his statutes, and to keep his commandments, as at this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v62" data-v="62"> And the king, and all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; with him, offered sacrifice before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v63" data-v="63"> And Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; offered a sacrifice of peace offerings, which he offered unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), two and twenty thousand oxen, and an hundred and twenty thousand sheep. So the king and all the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; dedicated the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v64" data-v="64"> The same day did the king hallow (to approve, commend, accept) the middle of the court that &#91;was&#93; before the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): for there he offered burnt offerings, and meat offerings, and the fat of the peace offerings: because the brazen altar that &#91;was&#93; before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;was&#93; too little to receive the burnt offerings, and meat offerings, and the fat of the peace offerings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v65" data-v="65"> And at that time Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; held a feast, and all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; with him, a great congregation, from the entering in of Hamat &#91;Fortress&#93; unto the river of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, seven days and seven days, &#91;even&#93; fourteen days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v66" data-v="66"> On the eighth day he sent the people away: and they Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the king, and went unto their tents joyful and glad of heart for all the goodness that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had done for David &#91;beloved&#93; his servant, and for Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; his people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="1 Kings 4:18; 2 Samuel 23:8–39; 2 Samuel 20:25"> And at this house, &#91;which&#93; is high, everyone that passes by it shall be astonished, and shall hiss; and they shall say, Why has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) done thus unto this land, and to this house?</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Job 15:21–22; Luke 17:26–29; Acts 21:31"> And Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; the son of Asa &#91;healer; injurious?&#93; began to reign over Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; in the fourth year of Ach’av &#91;father’s brother&#93; king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42" data-xref="2 Samuel 15:36; 2 Samuel 17:17; 2 Samuel 18:27"> Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; &#91;was&#93; thirty and five years old when he began to reign; and he reigned twenty and five years in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;. And his mother’s name &#91;was&#93; ‘Azuvah &#91;forsaken&#93; the daughter of Shilchi &#91;armed&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And he walked in all the ways of Asa &#91;healer; injurious?&#93; his father; he turned not aside from it, doing &#91;that which was&#93; right in the eyes of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): nevertheless the high places were not taken away; &#91;for&#93; the people offered and burnt incense yet in the high places.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; made peace with the king of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45" data-xref="1 Kings 1:40; Daniel 5:26–28; 1 Samuel 4:5"> Now the rest of the acts of Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93;, and his might that he showed, and how he warred, &#91;are&#93; they not written in the book of the Chronicles of the Kings of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46" data-xref="1 Chronicles 29:23; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11; 1 Kings 1:13"> And the remnant of the sodomites, which remained in the days of his father Asa &#91;healer; injurious?&#93;, he took out of the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47" data-xref="Genesis 47:31; 1 Kings 1:37; Ezra 6:10"> &#91;There was&#93; then no king in Edom &#91;Red&#93;: a deputy &#91;was&#93; king.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48" data-xref="1 Kings 3:6; Tehillim - Psalms 132:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 145:2"> Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; made ships of Tarshish &#91;yellow jasper&#93; to go to Ofir &#91;reducing to ashes&#93; for gold: but they went not; for the ships were broken at ‘Etzyon-Gever &#91;backbone of a man&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49" data-xref="Isaiah 21:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 28:1; Daniel 5:4–6"> Then said Achazyah &#91;Yehovah possesses&#93; the son of Ach’av &#91;father’s brother&#93; unto Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93;, Let my servants go with youri servants in the ships. But Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; would not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50" data-xref="1 Kings 2:28; Exodus 21:14; Exodus 27:2"> And Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; slept with his fathers, and was buried with his fathers in the city of David &#91;beloved&#93; his father: and Y’horam &#91;Yehovah is exalted&#93; his son reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> Achazyah &#91;Yehovah possesses&#93; the son of Ach’av &#91;father’s brother&#93; began to reign over Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93; the seventeenth year of Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93; king of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and reigned two years over Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52" data-xref="1 Samuel 14:45; 2 Samuel 14:11; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:6"> And he did evil in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and walked in the way of his father, and in the way of his mother, and in the way of Yarov’am &#91;the people will contend&#93; the son of N’vat &#91;aspect&#93;, who made Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to sin:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53" data-xref="1 Kings 1:16; 2 Samuel 14:24; 2 Samuel 1:2"> For he served Ba’al &#91;lord (a false god)&#93;, and worshipped him, and provoked to anger the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, according to all that his father had done.</span>
</div>
</div>

</div>
